import { useEffect, useState, useMemo } from "react";
import { Link, useLocation } from "wouter";
import { customFetch } from "@workspace/api-client-react";
import { Button } from "@/components/ui/button";
import { Badge } from "@/components/ui/badge";
import { Input } from "@/components/ui/input";
import { Plus, FileText, Trash2, ChevronRight, Search, X, Wrench, Link2, ScanLine } from "lucide-react";
import { ScanDialog, ScanResult } from "@/components/ScanDialog";
import { useToast } from "@/hooks/use-toast";
import { useUser } from "@clerk/react";
import {
  AlertDialog,
  AlertDialogAction,
  AlertDialogCancel,
  AlertDialogContent,
  AlertDialogDescription,
  AlertDialogFooter,
  AlertDialogHeader,
  AlertDialogTitle,
} from "@/components/ui/alert-dialog";
import {
  Select,
  SelectContent,
  SelectItem,
  SelectTrigger,
  SelectValue,
} from "@/components/ui/select";

type SpecSummary = {
  id: number;
  typ: string;
  hyvlingNr: number | null;
  specNr: number | null;
  kund: string | null;
  trailerNr: string | null;
  orderNr: string | null;
  status: string;
  bekraftadAt: string | null;
  skapadAt: string;
  antalPaket: number;
  totalM3: number;
  kopplatSpecId: number | null;
};

type TreeRow = {
  spec: SpecSummary;
  depth: number;
};

function formatDate(iso: string): string {
  return new Date(iso).toLocaleDateString("sv-SE", { day: "numeric", month: "short", year: "numeric" });
}

function buildTree(specs: SpecSummary[]): TreeRow[] {
  const byId = new Map(specs.map(s => [s.id, s]));
  const childrenOf = new Map<number, SpecSummary[]>();
  const childIds = new Set<number>();

  for (const spec of specs) {
    if (spec.kopplatSpecId && byId.has(spec.kopplatSpecId)) {
      const existing = childrenOf.get(spec.kopplatSpecId) ?? [];
      existing.push(spec);
      childrenOf.set(spec.kopplatSpecId, existing);
      childIds.add(spec.id);
    }
  }

  const roots = specs.filter(s => !childIds.has(s.id));
  const result: TreeRow[] = [];

  function addSpec(spec: SpecSummary, depth: number) {
    result.push({ spec, depth });
    const children = childrenOf.get(spec.id) ?? [];
    children.forEach(child => addSpec(child, depth + 1));
  }

  roots.forEach(r => addSpec(r, 0));
  return result;
}

type Props = { typ: "leverans" | "hyvling" };

export default function SpecList({ typ }: Props) {
  const [specs, setSpecs] = useState<SpecSummary[]>([]);
  const [loading, setLoading] = useState(true);
  const [deleteTarget, setDeleteTarget] = useState<SpecSummary | null>(null);
  const { toast } = useToast();
  const { user } = useUser();
  const [, navigate] = useLocation();

  const role = (user?.publicMetadata?.role as string) ?? null;
  const isAdmin = role !== "anvandare";

  const [searchKund, setSearchKund] = useState("");
  const [searchStatus, setSearchStatus] = useState("alla");
  const [dateFrom, setDateFrom] = useState("");
  const [dateTo, setDateTo] = useState("");
  const [scanOpen, setScanOpen] = useState(false);

  const basePath = typ === "hyvling" ? "/hyvling" : "/spec";
  const isHyvling = typ === "hyvling";

  async function load() {
    setLoading(true);
    const data = await customFetch<SpecSummary[]>(`/api/spec?typ=${typ}`);
    setSpecs(data);
    setLoading(false);
  }

  useEffect(() => { load(); }, [typ]);

  async function handleScanForSpec(scanResult: ScanResult) {
    const resp = await customFetch<{ specId: number; antalPaket: number }>("/api/spec/scan-import", {
      method: "POST",
      headers: { "Content-Type": "application/json" },
      body: JSON.stringify({
        avsandare: scanResult.avsandare,
        datum: scanResult.datum,
        paket: scanResult.paket,
      }),
    });
    toast({ title: `Spec skapad med ${resp.antalPaket} paket` });
    navigate(`${basePath}/${resp.specId}`);
  }

  async function confirmDelete() {
    if (!deleteTarget) return;
    await customFetch(`/api/spec/${deleteTarget.id}`, { method: "DELETE" });
    const nr = isHyvling ? (deleteTarget.hyvlingNr ?? deleteTarget.id) : (deleteTarget.specNr ?? deleteTarget.id);
    toast({ title: `${isHyvling ? "Hyvlingsorder" : "Spec"} #${nr} raderad` });
    setDeleteTarget(null);
    load();
  }

  const filtered = useMemo(() => {
    return specs.filter(s => {
      if (searchKund && !((s.kund ?? "").toLowerCase().includes(searchKund.toLowerCase()))) return false;
      if (searchStatus === "utkast" && s.status !== "utkast") return false;
      if (searchStatus === "bekraftad" && s.status !== "bekraftad") return false;
      if (dateFrom && s.skapadAt < dateFrom) return false;
      if (dateTo && s.skapadAt > dateTo + "T23:59:59") return false;
      return true;
    });
  }, [specs, searchKund, searchStatus, dateFrom, dateTo]);

  const hasFilter = !!(searchKund || searchStatus !== "alla" || dateFrom || dateTo);

  // Tree when no filter, flat when filtering
  const treeRows = useMemo(() => {
    if (hasFilter) return filtered.map(s => ({ spec: s, depth: 0 }));
    return buildTree(specs);
  }, [specs, filtered, hasFilter]);

  function clearFilters() {
    setSearchKund("");
    setSearchStatus("alla");
    setDateFrom("");
    setDateTo("");
  }

  const Icon = isHyvling ? Wrench : FileText;

  return (
    <div className="space-y-6">
      <div className="flex items-center justify-between gap-2">
        <div>
          <h1 className="text-2xl sm:text-3xl font-bold tracking-tight">
            {isHyvling ? "Hyvlingsordrar" : "Specifikationer"}
          </h1>
          <p className="text-muted-foreground text-sm hidden sm:block">
            {isHyvling
              ? "Sågade paket som ska hyvlas — avregistreras ur lagret som sågat."
              : "Utkast och bekräftade följesedlar för kundleveranser."}
          </p>
        </div>
        <div className="flex gap-2">
          <Button variant="outline" size="sm" className="gap-1.5" onClick={() => setScanOpen(true)}>
            <ScanLine className="h-4 w-4" />
            <span className="hidden sm:inline">Skanna</span>
          </Button>
          <Link href={`${basePath}/ny`}>
            <Button size="sm" className="gap-1.5">
              <Plus className="h-4 w-4" />
              {isHyvling ? "Ny order" : "Ny Spec"}
            </Button>
          </Link>
        </div>
      </div>

      {/* Filterrad */}
      <div className="flex flex-wrap gap-2 items-center">
        <div className="relative flex-1 min-w-[180px]">
          <Search className="absolute left-2.5 top-2.5 h-4 w-4 text-muted-foreground" />
          <Input
            className="pl-9"
            placeholder={isHyvling ? "Sök anteckning..." : "Sök köpare..."}
            value={searchKund}
            onChange={e => setSearchKund(e.target.value)}
          />
        </div>
        <Select value={searchStatus} onValueChange={setSearchStatus}>
          <SelectTrigger className="w-[140px]">
            <SelectValue />
          </SelectTrigger>
          <SelectContent>
            <SelectItem value="alla">Alla status</SelectItem>
            <SelectItem value="utkast">Utkast</SelectItem>
            <SelectItem value="bekraftad">Bekräftad</SelectItem>
          </SelectContent>
        </Select>
        <Input
          type="date"
          className="w-[140px]"
          value={dateFrom}
          onChange={e => setDateFrom(e.target.value)}
          title="Från datum"
        />
        <Input
          type="date"
          className="w-[140px]"
          value={dateTo}
          onChange={e => setDateTo(e.target.value)}
          title="Till datum"
        />
        {hasFilter && (
          <Button variant="ghost" size="sm" onClick={clearFilters} className="gap-1 text-muted-foreground">
            <X className="h-4 w-4" /> Rensa
          </Button>
        )}
        {hasFilter && (
          <span className="text-sm text-muted-foreground">{filtered.length} av {specs.length}</span>
        )}
      </div>

      {loading ? (
        <div className="text-muted-foreground text-sm">Laddar...</div>
      ) : treeRows.length === 0 ? (
        <div className="text-center py-20 text-muted-foreground">
          <Icon className="h-12 w-12 mx-auto mb-3 opacity-30" />
          <p className="font-medium">
            {specs.length === 0
              ? `Inga ${isHyvling ? "hyvlingsordrar" : "specifikationer"} ännu`
              : "Inga poster matchar filtret"}
          </p>
          {specs.length === 0 && (
            <p className="text-sm mt-1">
              Klicka "{isHyvling ? "Ny order" : "Ny Spec"}" för att skapa din första.
            </p>
          )}
        </div>
      ) : (
        <>
          {/* ── Mobilkortvy ── */}
          <div className="sm:hidden space-y-2">
            {treeRows.map(({ spec: s, depth }) => {
              const statusBadge = s.status === "bekraftad"
                ? <Badge className={isHyvling ? "bg-orange-100 text-orange-800 border-orange-200" : "bg-green-100 text-green-800 border-green-200"}>{isHyvling ? "Hyvlad" : "Bekräftad"}</Badge>
                : <Badge variant="outline">Utkast</Badge>;
              return (
                <div
                  key={s.id}
                  className={`border rounded-lg p-3 bg-card cursor-pointer active:bg-muted/30 ${depth > 0 ? "ml-4 border-l-2 border-l-muted-foreground/20" : ""}`}
                  onClick={() => navigate(`${basePath}/${s.id}`)}
                >
                  <div className="flex items-start justify-between gap-2">
                    <div className="flex-1 min-w-0">
                      <div className="flex items-center gap-2 flex-wrap mb-1">
                        {statusBadge}
                        <span className={`font-medium text-sm truncate ${depth > 0 ? "text-muted-foreground" : ""}`}>
                          {s.kund || <span className="text-muted-foreground italic text-xs">Ingen köpare</span>}
                          {s.kopplatSpecId && !hasFilter && <Link2 className="inline h-3 w-3 ml-1 text-muted-foreground/40" />}
                        </span>
                      </div>
                      <div className="text-xs text-muted-foreground space-y-0.5">
                        {!isHyvling && (s.trailerNr || s.orderNr) && (
                          <div className="flex gap-3 flex-wrap">
                            {s.trailerNr && <span>Trailer: {s.trailerNr}</span>}
                            {s.orderNr && <span>Order: {s.orderNr}</span>}
                          </div>
                        )}
                        <div className="flex gap-3 flex-wrap">
                          <span>{formatDate(s.skapadAt)}</span>
                          {s.antalPaket > 0 && <span className="font-medium text-foreground">{s.antalPaket} paket</span>}
                          {s.totalM3 > 0 && <span className="font-medium text-foreground">{Number(s.totalM3).toFixed(2)} m³</span>}
                        </div>
                      </div>
                    </div>
                    <div className="flex items-center gap-1 shrink-0">
                      <span className="text-xs text-muted-foreground font-mono">#{isHyvling ? (s.hyvlingNr ?? s.id) : (s.specNr ?? s.id)}</span>
                      {isAdmin && (
                        <button
                          onClick={(e) => { e.preventDefault(); e.stopPropagation(); setDeleteTarget(s); }}
                          className="p-1.5 rounded hover:bg-destructive/10 text-muted-foreground hover:text-destructive"
                        >
                          <Trash2 className="h-3.5 w-3.5" />
                        </button>
                      )}
                      <ChevronRight className="h-4 w-4 text-muted-foreground" />
                    </div>
                  </div>
                </div>
              );
            })}
          </div>

          {/* ── Desktoptabell ── */}
          <div className="hidden sm:block rounded-lg border overflow-hidden">
            <table className="w-full text-sm">
              <thead className="bg-muted/50 border-b">
                <tr>
                  <th className="text-left px-4 py-3 font-medium text-muted-foreground">#</th>
                  <th className="text-left px-4 py-3 font-medium text-muted-foreground">
                    {isHyvling ? "Anteckning" : "Köpare"}
                  </th>
                  {!isHyvling && <th className="text-left px-4 py-3 font-medium text-muted-foreground">Trailer</th>}
                  {!isHyvling && <th className="text-left px-4 py-3 font-medium text-muted-foreground">Order</th>}
                  <th className="text-right px-4 py-3 font-medium text-muted-foreground">Paket</th>
                  <th className="text-right px-4 py-3 font-medium text-muted-foreground">Nom m³</th>
                  <th className="text-left px-4 py-3 font-medium text-muted-foreground">Skapad</th>
                  <th className="text-left px-4 py-3 font-medium text-muted-foreground">Status</th>
                  <th className="px-4 py-3"></th>
                </tr>
              </thead>
              <tbody>
                {treeRows.map(({ spec: s, depth }) => (
                  <tr
                    key={s.id}
                    className={`border-b last:border-0 hover:bg-muted/30 cursor-pointer ${depth > 0 ? "bg-muted/10" : ""}`}
                    onClick={() => navigate(`${basePath}/${s.id}`)}
                  >
                    <td className="px-4 py-3 text-muted-foreground font-mono">
                      <div className="flex items-center gap-1" style={{ paddingLeft: depth * 20 }}>
                        {depth > 0 && (
                          <span className="text-muted-foreground/40 shrink-0 select-none">↳</span>
                        )}
                        {isHyvling ? (s.hyvlingNr ?? s.id) : (s.specNr ?? s.id)}
                      </div>
                    </td>
                    <td className="px-4 py-3">
                      <div className="flex items-center gap-1.5">
                        <span className={depth > 0 ? "font-normal text-muted-foreground" : "font-medium"}>
                          {s.kund || <span className="text-muted-foreground">–</span>}
                        </span>
                        {s.kopplatSpecId && !hasFilter && (
                          <Link2 className="h-3 w-3 text-muted-foreground/40 shrink-0" title="Kopplad till annan spec" />
                        )}
                      </div>
                    </td>
                    {!isHyvling && <td className="px-4 py-3">{s.trailerNr || <span className="text-muted-foreground">–</span>}</td>}
                    {!isHyvling && <td className="px-4 py-3">{s.orderNr || <span className="text-muted-foreground">–</span>}</td>}
                    <td className="px-4 py-3 text-right tabular-nums">{s.antalPaket}</td>
                    <td className="px-4 py-3 text-right tabular-nums font-medium">
                      {s.totalM3 > 0 ? Number(s.totalM3).toFixed(2) : <span className="text-muted-foreground">–</span>}
                    </td>
                    <td className="px-4 py-3 text-muted-foreground">{formatDate(s.skapadAt)}</td>
                    <td className="px-4 py-3">
                      {s.status === "bekraftad" ? (
                        <Badge className={isHyvling
                          ? "bg-orange-100 text-orange-800 border-orange-200"
                          : "bg-green-100 text-green-800 border-green-200"
                        }>
                          {isHyvling ? "Hyvlad" : "Bekräftad"}
                        </Badge>
                      ) : (
                        <Badge variant="outline">Utkast</Badge>
                      )}
                    </td>
                    <td className="px-4 py-3">
                      <div className="flex items-center gap-1 justify-end">
                        {isAdmin && (
                          <button
                            onClick={(e) => { e.preventDefault(); e.stopPropagation(); setDeleteTarget(s); }}
                            className="p-1.5 rounded hover:bg-destructive/10 text-muted-foreground hover:text-destructive"
                            title="Radera"
                          >
                            <Trash2 className="h-3.5 w-3.5" />
                          </button>
                        )}
                        <ChevronRight className="h-4 w-4 text-muted-foreground" />
                      </div>
                    </td>
                  </tr>
                ))}
              </tbody>
            </table>
          </div>
        </>
      )}

      <AlertDialog open={!!deleteTarget} onOpenChange={(open) => { if (!open) setDeleteTarget(null); }}>
        <AlertDialogContent>
          <AlertDialogHeader>
            <AlertDialogTitle>
              Radera {isHyvling ? "Hyvlingsorder" : "Spec"} #{isHyvling ? (deleteTarget?.hyvlingNr ?? deleteTarget?.id) : (deleteTarget?.specNr ?? deleteTarget?.id)}?
            </AlertDialogTitle>
            <AlertDialogDescription>
              {deleteTarget?.status === "bekraftad" ? (
                <>
                  Den här ordern är <strong>bekräftad</strong> och paketen är markerade som utlevererade.
                  {" "}Radering tar bort ordern men <strong>återställer inte</strong> paketen — det görs via "Återöppna".
                </>
              ) : (
                "Ordern raderas permanent. Paketen påverkas inte."
              )}
            </AlertDialogDescription>
          </AlertDialogHeader>
          <AlertDialogFooter>
            <AlertDialogCancel>Avbryt</AlertDialogCancel>
            <AlertDialogAction
              className="bg-destructive text-destructive-foreground hover:bg-destructive/90"
              onClick={confirmDelete}
            >
              Radera
            </AlertDialogAction>
          </AlertDialogFooter>
        </AlertDialogContent>
      </AlertDialog>

      <ScanDialog
        open={scanOpen}
        onOpenChange={setScanOpen}
        onPackagesConfirmed={handleScanForSpec}
      />
    </div>
  );
}

  pktNr: string | null;

const CERT_OPTIONS = [
  {
    id: "pefc",
    label: "PEFC",
    nr: "PBN-PEFC-COC-078374",
    lines: [
      "Products/wood on this invoice is 100% PEFC certified. Certificate No. PBN-PEFC-COC-078374",
    ],
  },
  {
    id: "fsc",
    label: "FSC Controlled Wood",
    nr: "PBN-CW-078374",
    lines: [
      "FSC Controlled Wood. Certifikat Nr. PBN-CW-078374",
    ],
  },
  {
    id: "kd",
    label: "KD/ISPM 15",
    nr: "SE-H7883",
    lines: [
      "Allt virke i denna leverans är behandlat enligt KD 56 grader C/30 min, i enlighet med ISPM 15. Registreringsnr SE-H7883",
      "This timber is heat treated to a core temperature of 56 degrees for a minimum period of 30 minutes according to ISPM 15. Cert.No: SE-H7883",
    ],
  },
] as const;

function certPrintText(certs: string[]): React.ReactNode {
  const sel = CERT_OPTIONS.filter(c => certs.includes(c.id));
  if (sel.length === 0) return null;
  return sel.flatMap(c => c.lines).map((line, i) => <div key={i}>{line}</div>);
}

type SpecSearchItem = {
  id: number;
  typ: string;
  hyvlingNr: number | null;
  specNr: number | null;
  kund: string | null;
  status: string;
  antalPaket: number;
  kopplatSpecId: number | null;
};

function useDebounce<T>(value: T, delay = 300): T {
  const [d, setD] = useState(value);
  useEffect(() => {
    const id = setTimeout(() => setD(value), delay);
    return () => clearTimeout(id);
  }, [value, delay]);
  return d;
}

function dimStr(t: number | null, b: number | null) {
  if (!t && !b) return "-";
  return `${t ?? "-"}×${b ?? "-"}`;
}

function computedNomM3(p: Pkg): number | null {
  const nomT = p.nomTjocklek;
  const nomB = p.nomBredd;
  // Bäst: nom dim × lpm (sparad)
  if (nomT && nomB && p.lpm) {
    return parseFloat(((nomT / 1000) * (nomB / 1000) * p.lpm).toFixed(4));
  }
  // Fallback: beräkna lpm från langdDm × antal om lpm saknas
  if (nomT && nomB && p.langdDm && p.antal) {
    const lpm = (p.langdDm / 10) * p.antal;
    return parseFloat(((nomT / 1000) * (nomB / 1000) * lpm).toFixed(4));
  }
  // Sista utväg: sparat m3-värde
  return p.m3;
}

function computedActM3(p: Pkg): number | null {
  if (p.actM3 != null && p.actM3 > 0) return p.actM3;
  if (p.actTjocklek && p.actBredd && p.lpm) {
    return (p.actTjocklek / 1000) * (p.actBredd / 1000) * p.lpm;
  }
  return null;
}

export default function SpecPage({ typ: typProp = "leverans" }: { typ?: "leverans" | "hyvling" }) {
  const params = useParams<{ id?: string }>();
  const specId = params.id && params.id !== "ny" ? parseInt(params.id) : null;
  const [, navigate] = useLocation();
  const { toast } = useToast();
  const queryClient = useQueryClient();
  const branding = useBranding();
  const companyName = branding.companyName;
  const [documentSenderSnapshot, setDocumentSenderSnapshot] = useState<Branding | null>(null);
  const documentSender = documentSenderSnapshot ?? branding;
  const logoSrc = documentSender.logoUrl;
  const senderLocation = [documentSender.postalCode, documentSender.city].filter(Boolean).join(" ");
  const senderFooter = [
    documentSender.companyName,
    documentSender.streetAddress,
    senderLocation,
    documentSender.country,
    documentSender.phone ? `Tel: ${documentSender.phone}` : "",
    documentSender.email,
    documentSender.website,
  ].filter(Boolean).join(" · ");

  const [typ, setTyp] = useState<"leverans" | "hyvling">(typProp);
  const isHyvling = typ === "hyvling";
  const basePath = isHyvling ? "/hyvling" : "/spec";

  const [kund, setKund] = useState("");
  const [kunder, setKunder] = useState<{ id: number; namn: string }[]>([]);
  const [adress, setAdress] = useState("");
  const [trailerNr, setTrailerNr] = useState("");
  const [orderNr, setOrderNr] = useState("");
  const [erReferens, setErReferens] = useState("");
  const [anteckning, setAnteckning] = useState("");
  const [certifikat, setCertifikat] = useState<string[]>([]);
  const [showCertificationOnDocument, setShowCertificationOnDocument] = useState(false);
  const [hyvlingNr, setHyvlingNr] = useState<number | null>(null);
  const [specNr, setSpecNr] = useState<number | null>(null);
  const [kopplatSpecId, setKopplatSpecId] = useState<number | null>(null);
  const [kopplatSpec, setKopplatSpec] = useState<SpecLink | null>(null);
  const [reverseLinks, setReverseLinks] = useState<SpecLink[]>([]);
  const [alleSpecs, setAlleSpecs] = useState<SpecSearchItem[]>([]);
  const [specSokQuery, setSpecSokQuery] = useState("");
  const [specPickerOpen, setSpecPickerOpen] = useState(false);

  // Visningsnummer: eget löpnummer för hyvling och leverans
  const displayNr = isHyvling ? hyvlingNr : specNr;
  const [selected, setSelected] = useState<Map<number, Pkg>>(new Map());
  const [status, setStatus] = useState("utkast");
  const [saving, setSaving] = useState(false);
  const [confirming, setConfirming] = useState(false);
  const [ateropping, setAteropping] = useState(false);
  const [deleting, setDeleting] = useState(false);
  const [konverterar, setKonverterar] = useState(false);
  const [konvertararTillImp, setKonvertararTillImp] = useState(false);
  const [loadingSpec, setLoadingSpec] = useState(false);
  const [search, setSearch] = useState("");
  const debouncedSearch = useDebounce(search, 300);
  const [skannaOpen, setSkannaOpen] = useState(false);
  const [visaPrisIPrint, setVisaPrisIPrint] = useState(false);
  const [tillampingPrislista, setTillampingPrislista] = useState(false);
  const [prislistaPopoverOpen, setPrislistaPopoverOpen] = useState(false);
  const [allaPrislistor, setAllaPrislistor] = useState<{ id: number; namn: string; kund: string | null; datum: string | null }[]>([]);
  const [valdPrislistaId, setValdPrislistaId] = useState<number | null>(null);
  const [laderPrislistor, setLaderPrislistor] = useState(false);
  const [editingPriceId, setEditingPriceId] = useState<number | null>(null);
  const [editingPriceVal, setEditingPriceVal] = useState("");
  const [kollisioner, setKollisioner] = useState<Map<number, { specId: number; kund: string | null; status: string }[]>>(new Map());
  const [pdfLoading, setPdfLoading] = useState(false);
  const confirmAndPrintRef = useRef(createConfirmAndPrintRunner());
  const confirmInFlightRef = useRef(false);

  async function checkKollision(lagerIds: number[]): Promise<Map<number, { specId: number; kund: string | null; status: string }[]>> {
    if (lagerIds.length === 0) return new Map();
    const params = new URLSearchParams({ lagerIds: lagerIds.join(",") });
    if (specId) params.set("excludeSpecId", String(specId));
    try {
      const data = await customFetch<{ kollisioner: { lagerId: number; specId: number; kund: string | null; status: string }[] }>(`/api/spec/kollision?${params}`);
      const map = new Map<number, { specId: number; kund: string | null; status: string }[]>();
      for (const k of data.kollisioner) {
        const arr = map.get(k.lagerId) ?? [];
        arr.push({ specId: k.specId, kund: k.kund, status: k.status });
        map.set(k.lagerId, arr);
      }
      return map;
    } catch {
      return new Map();
    }
  }

  // Hämta sparade köpare för autocomplete
  useEffect(() => {
    customFetch<{ id: number; namn: string }[]>("/api/kunder").then(setKunder).catch(() => {});
    // Hämta alla specs för kopplad-spec-picker
    customFetch<SpecSearchItem[]>("/api/spec").then(setAlleSpecs).catch(() => {});
  }, []);

  // Load existing spec
  useEffect(() => {
    if (!specId) return;
    setLoadingSpec(true);
    customFetch<SavedSpec>(`/api/spec/${specId}`)
      .then(async (spec) => {
        setKund(spec.kund ?? "");
        setAdress(spec.adress ?? "");
        setTrailerNr(spec.trailerNr ?? "");
        setOrderNr(spec.orderNr ?? "");
        setErReferens(spec.erReferens ?? "");
        setAnteckning(spec.anteckning ?? "");
        setCertifikat(spec.certifikat ?? []);
        setShowCertificationOnDocument(spec.showCertificationOnDocument === true);
        setDocumentSenderSnapshot(spec.documentSenderSnapshot ? normalizeBranding(spec.documentSenderSnapshot) : null);
        setHyvlingNr(spec.hyvlingNr ?? null);
        setSpecNr(spec.specNr ?? null);
        setStatus(spec.status);
        setKopplatSpecId(spec.kopplatSpecId ?? null);
        setKopplatSpec(spec.kopplatSpec ?? null);
        setReverseLinks(spec.reverseLinks ?? []);
        if (spec.typ === "hyvling" || spec.typ === "leverans") setTyp(spec.typ);
        const map = new Map<number, Pkg>();
        for (const p of spec.paket) map.set(p.id, p);
        setSelected(map);
        if (spec.paket.length > 0) {
          const k = await checkKollision(spec.paket.map(p => p.id));
          setKollisioner(k);
        }
      })
      .finally(() => setLoadingSpec(false));
  }, [specId]);

  const { data: searchResult } = useListLagerPoster(
    { search: debouncedSearch || undefined, status: "i_lager", limit: 200 },
    {
      query: {
        queryKey: getListLagerPosterQueryKey({ search: debouncedSearch || undefined, status: "i_lager", limit: 200 }),
        enabled: debouncedSearch.length >= 1,
      },
    }
  );

  const searchPkgs: Pkg[] = useMemo(() => {
    const raw = (searchResult?.data ?? []) as unknown as Pkg[];

    return [...raw].sort((a, b) => {
      const aNum = parseInt(a.pktNr ?? "", 10);
      const bNum = parseInt(b.pktNr ?? "", 10);
      if (!isNaN(aNum) && !isNaN(bNum)) return aNum - bNum;
      return (a.pktNr ?? "").localeCompare(b.pktNr ?? "", "sv", { numeric: true });
    });
  }, [searchResult, debouncedSearch]);
  type SpecSortKey = "pktNr" | "profil" | "traslag" | "nomDim" | "langdDm" | "antal" | "m3" | "actM3";
  type SortEntry = { key: SpecSortKey; dir: "asc" | "desc" };
  const [specSortKeys, setSpecSortKeys] = useState<SortEntry[]>([]);

  function cmpByKey(a: Pkg, b: Pkg, key: SpecSortKey): number {
    if (key === "pktNr") {
      const an = parseInt(a.pktNr ?? "", 10), bn = parseInt(b.pktNr ?? "", 10);
      return (!isNaN(an) && !isNaN(bn)) ? an - bn : (a.pktNr ?? "").localeCompare(b.pktNr ?? "", "sv", { numeric: true });
    }
    if (key === "profil") return (a.profil ?? "").localeCompare(b.profil ?? "", "sv", { sensitivity: "base" });
    if (key === "traslag") {
      const aStr = [a.traslag, a.profil].filter(Boolean).join(" / ");
      const bStr = [b.traslag, b.profil].filter(Boolean).join(" / ");
      return aStr.localeCompare(bStr, "sv", { sensitivity: "base" });
    }
    if (key === "nomDim") return ((a.nomTjocklek ?? 0) * 10000 + (a.nomBredd ?? 0)) - ((b.nomTjocklek ?? 0) * 10000 + (b.nomBredd ?? 0));
    if (key === "langdDm") return (a.langdDm ?? 0) - (b.langdDm ?? 0);
    if (key === "antal") return (a.antal ?? 0) - (b.antal ?? 0);
    if (key === "m3") return (a.m3 ?? 0) - (b.m3 ?? 0);
    if (key === "actM3") return ((a.actM3 ?? a.m3 ?? 0)) - ((b.actM3 ?? b.m3 ?? 0));
    return 0;
  }

  function handleSpecSort(key: SpecSortKey, additive: boolean) {
    setSpecSortKeys(prev => {
      const idx = prev.findIndex(e => e.key === key);
      if (idx !== -1) {
        // Toggle direction on already-sorted column
        return prev.map((e, i) => i === idx ? { ...e, dir: e.dir === "asc" ? "desc" : "asc" } : e);
      }
      if (additive && prev.length > 0) {
        // Shift-click: append as next level (max 3)
        return prev.length >= 3 ? prev : [...prev, { key, dir: "asc" }];
      }
      // Regular click on a new column: make it primary, keep existing as secondary (max 3 total)
      const rest = prev.slice(0, 2);
      return [{ key, dir: "asc" }, ...rest];
    });
  }

  const selectedList = useMemo(() => {
    const list = Array.from(selected.values());
    if (specSortKeys.length === 0) return list;
    return [...list].sort((a, b) => {
      for (const { key, dir } of specSortKeys) {
        const cmp = cmpByKey(a, b, key);
        if (cmp !== 0) return dir === "asc" ? cmp : -cmp;
      }
      return 0;
    });
  }, [selected, specSortKeys]);

  const selectedPackageCount = useMemo(() => {
    return new Set(selectedList.map(p => p.pktNr ? `pkt:${p.pktNr}` : `rad:${p.id}`)).size;
  }, [selectedList]);

  const lengths = useMemo(() => {
    const s = new Set<number>();
    selectedList.forEach(p => { if (p.langdDm) s.add(p.langdDm); });
    return Array.from(s).sort((a, b) => a - b);
  }, [selectedList]);

  const totals = useMemo(() => {
    let styck = 0, lpm = 0, m3 = 0, nomM3 = 0, actM3 = 0, saljSum = 0;
    const perLength: Record<number, number> = {};
    for (const p of selectedList) {
      styck += p.antal ?? 0;
      lpm += p.lpm ?? 0;
      m3 += p.m3 ?? 0;
      nomM3 += computedNomM3(p) ?? 0;
      actM3 += computedActM3(p) ?? 0;
      saljSum += p.saljSum ?? 0;
      if (p.langdDm) perLength[p.langdDm] = (perLength[p.langdDm] ?? 0) + (p.antal ?? 0);
    }
    return { styck, lpm, m3, nomM3, actM3, saljSum, perLength };
  }, [selectedList]);

  const hasPris = !isHyvling && selectedList.length > 0;
  const hasVilmabas = selectedList.some(p => p.vilmabasId);

  async function savePris(pkgId: number, valStr: string) {
    setEditingPriceId(null);
    if (!specId) return;
    const parsed = valStr.trim().replace(",", ".");
    const num = parsed === "" ? null : parseFloat(parsed);
    if (num !== null && isNaN(num)) return;
    try {
      const data = await customFetch<{ saljPris: number | null; saljSum: number | null }>(
        `/api/spec/${specId}/paket/${pkgId}/pris`,
        { method: "PATCH", headers: { "Content-Type": "application/json" }, body: JSON.stringify({ saljPris: num }) }
      );
      setSelected(prev => {
        const n = new Map(prev);
        const p = n.get(pkgId);
        if (p) n.set(pkgId, { ...p, saljPris: data.saljPris, saljSum: data.saljSum });
        return n;
      });
    } catch {
      toast({ title: "Kunde inte spara pris", variant: "destructive" });
    }
  }

  async function oppnaPrislistaPopover() {
    setPrislistaPopoverOpen(true);
    if (allaPrislistor.length > 0) return;
    setLaderPrislistor(true);
    try {
      const data = await customFetch<{ id: number; namn: string; kund: string | null; datum: string | null }[]>("/api/prislistor");
      setAllaPrislistor(data);
      // Pre-select kundens senaste prislista
      if (kund && valdPrislistaId == null) {
        const kundListor = data.filter(p => p.kund === kund);
        if (kundListor.length > 0) setValdPrislistaId(kundListor[kundListor.length - 1].id);
      }
    } catch {
      // ignore
    } finally {
      setLaderPrislistor(false);
    }
  }

  async function tillampPrislista() {
    if (!specId) return;
    setPrislistaPopoverOpen(false);
    setTillampingPrislista(true);
    try {
      const body: Record<string, unknown> = {};
      if (valdPrislistaId) body.prislistaId = valdPrislistaId;
      const data = await customFetch<{ matchade: number; ej_matchade: number; uppdaterade: number }>(`/api/spec/${specId}/tillamp-prislista`, {
        method: "POST",
        headers: { "Content-Type": "application/json" },
        body: JSON.stringify(body),
      });
      // Reload packages to get updated prices
      const spec = await customFetch<SavedSpec>(`/api/spec/${specId}`);
      const map = new Map<number, Pkg>();
      for (const p of spec.paket) map.set(p.id, p);
      setSelected(map);
      if (data.ej_matchade > 0) {
        toast({
          title: `Prislista tillämpad — ${data.matchade} av ${data.uppdaterade} paket matchade`,
          description: `${data.ej_matchade} paket saknar match i prislistan för "${kund}".`,
          variant: "destructive",
          duration: 6000,
        });
      } else {
        toast({ title: `Prislista tillämpad — alla ${data.matchade} paket prissatta` });
      }
    } catch {
      toast({ title: "Kunde inte tillämpa prislista", variant: "destructive" });
    } finally {
      setTillampingPrislista(false);
    }
  }

  async function addPkg(p: Pkg) {
    setSelected(prev => { const n = new Map(prev); n.set(p.id, p); return n; });
    const k = await checkKollision([p.id]);
    if (k.size > 0) {
      const hits = k.get(p.id) ?? [];
      setKollisioner(prev => { const n = new Map(prev); n.set(p.id, hits); return n; });
      const specNr = hits.map(h => `#${h.specId}${h.kund ? ` (${h.kund})` : ""}`).join(", ");
      const statusStr = hits.some(h => h.status === "bekraftad") ? " — minst en är bekräftad!" : "";
      toast({
        title: `⚠ Paket ${p.pktNr ?? p.id} finns redan på spec ${specNr}${statusStr}`,
        description: "Paketet är tillagt, men kontrollera att det inte levereras dubbelt.",
        variant: "destructive",
        duration: 8000,
      });
    } else {
      setKollisioner(prev => { const n = new Map(prev); n.delete(p.id); return n; });
    }
  }

  async function addPkgByPktNr(pktNr: string): Promise<KameraScanResult> {
    const normalizedPktNr = pktNr.trim();
    if (!normalizedPktNr) return { kind: "error", selectedCount: selectedPackageCount };

    if (Array.from(selected.values()).some(p => p.pktNr === normalizedPktNr)) {
      toast({
        title: `Paket ${normalizedPktNr} finns redan i Valda paket`,
        description: "Paketet lades inte till igen.",
        variant: "destructive",
      });
      return { kind: "duplicate", selectedCount: selectedPackageCount };
    }

    try {
      const paket = await customFetch<PaketSvar>(`/api/lager/paket/${encodeURIComponent(normalizedPktNr)}`);
      if (paket.rader.length === 0) {
        toast({ title: `Paket ${normalizedPktNr} hittades inte`, variant: "destructive" });
        return { kind: "not-found", selectedCount: selectedPackageCount };
      }

      const redanValdaId = new Set(Array.from(selected.keys()));
      const nyaRader = paket.rader.filter(p => !redanValdaId.has(p.id));
      if (nyaRader.length === 0) {
        toast({
          title: `Paket ${normalizedPktNr} finns redan i Valda paket`,
          description: "Paketet lades inte till igen.",
          variant: "destructive",
        });
        return { kind: "duplicate", selectedCount: selectedPackageCount };
      }

      setSelected(prev => {
        const n = new Map(prev);
        for (const p of nyaRader) n.set(p.id, p);
        return n;
      });

      const k = await checkKollision(nyaRader.map(p => p.id));
      if (k.size > 0) {
        setKollisioner(prev => {
          const n = new Map(prev);
          for (const [id, hits] of k) n.set(id, hits);
          return n;
        });
        const hits = Array.from(k.values()).flat();
        const specRefs = Array.from(new Map(hits.map(hit => [hit.specId, hit])).values())
          .map(hit => `#${hit.specId}${hit.kund ? ` (${hit.kund})` : ""}`)
          .join(", ");
        const statusStr = hits.some(hit => hit.status === "bekraftad") ? " Minst en är bekräftad." : "";
        toast({
          title: `⚠ Paket ${normalizedPktNr} finns redan på spec ${specRefs}`,
          description: `Paketet är tillagt, men kontrollera att det inte levereras dubbelt.${statusStr}`,
          variant: "destructive",
          duration: 8000,
        });
      } else {
        toast({
          title: `Paket ${normalizedPktNr} tillagt`,
          description: `${nyaRader.length} ${nyaRader.length === 1 ? "lagerpost" : "lagerposter"} lades till i Valda paket.`,
        });
      }
      return { kind: "added", selectedCount: selectedPackageCount + 1 };
    } catch (err) {
      toast({
        title: `Paket ${normalizedPktNr} hittades inte`,
        description: err instanceof Error ? err.message : "Paketet saknas i aktuellt lager.",
        variant: "destructive",
      });
      return { kind: "not-found", selectedCount: selectedPackageCount };
    }
  }

  function handleOklarKodFranScanner(raValde: string, kodTyp: string) {
    setSearch(raValde);
    toast({
      title: `Oklar kod (${kodTyp})`,
      description: "Koden kunde inte matchas säkert. Välj rätt paket i sökresultatet.",
      variant: "destructive",
    });
  }

  function removePkg(id: number) {
    setSelected(prev => { const n = new Map(prev); n.delete(id); return n; });
    setKollisioner(prev => { const n = new Map(prev); n.delete(id); return n; });
  }

  function specLabel(s: SpecLink | SpecSearchItem): string {
    const nr = s.typ === "hyvling" && s.hyvlingNr
      ? `Hyvl #${s.hyvlingNr}`
      : `Spec #${s.specNr ?? s.id}`;
    const kundStr = s.kund ? ` — ${s.kund}` : "";
    const typStr = s.typ === "hyvling" ? " (hyvling)" : " (leverans)";
    return `${nr}${kundStr}${typStr}`;
  }

  async function saveSpec(navigateAfterCreate = true): Promise<number | null> {
    setSaving(true);
    const body = { typ, kund: kund || null, adress: adress || null, trailerNr: trailerNr || null, orderNr: orderNr || null, erReferens: erReferens || null, anteckning: anteckning || null, kopplatSpecId: kopplatSpecId ?? null, certifikat, showCertificationOnDocument, paketIds: selectedList.map(p => p.id) };
    try {
      if (specId) {
        await customFetch(`/api/spec/${specId}`, { method: "PUT", headers: { "Content-Type": "application/json" }, body: JSON.stringify(body) });
        toast({ title: isHyvling ? "Hyvlingsorder sparad" : "Spec sparad" });
        return specId;
      } else {
        const data = await customFetch<{ id: number }>("/api/spec", { method: "POST", headers: { "Content-Type": "application/json" }, body: JSON.stringify(body) });
        toast({ title: isHyvling ? "Hyvlingsorder sparad som utkast" : "Spec sparad som utkast" });
        if (navigateAfterCreate) navigate(`${basePath}/${data.id}`);
        return data.id;
      }
    } catch {
      toast({ title: "Fel vid sparning", variant: "destructive" });
      return null;
    } finally {
      setSaving(false);
    }
  }

  function invalidateLagerCache() {
    queryClient.invalidateQueries({ queryKey: getGetLagerStatistikQueryKey() });
    queryClient.invalidateQueries({ queryKey: ["/api/lager/statistik"] });
    queryClient.invalidateQueries({ queryKey: ["/api/lager/leveranstrend"] });
    queryClient.invalidateQueries({ queryKey: ["/api/lager/lagertrend"] });
    queryClient.invalidateQueries({ queryKey: ["/api/lager"] });
    queryClient.invalidateQueries({ queryKey: ["/api/spec"] });
  }

  async function confirmSpec() {
    if (confirmInFlightRef.current) return;
    confirmInFlightRef.current = true;
    setConfirming(true);
    try {
      const result = await confirmAndPrintRef.current({
        save: () => saveSpec(false),
        confirm: id => customFetch<SavedSpec>(`/api/spec/${id}/bekrafta`, { method: "POST" }),
        onConfirmed: (id, confirmedSpec) => {
          setDocumentSenderSnapshot(confirmedSpec.documentSenderSnapshot
            ? normalizeBranding(confirmedSpec.documentSenderSnapshot)
            : null);
          setHyvlingNr(confirmedSpec.hyvlingNr ?? null);
          setSpecNr(confirmedSpec.specNr ?? null);
          setStatus("bekraftad");
          invalidateLagerCache();
          toast({ title: isHyvling
            ? `Hyvlingsorder #${id} bekräftad — ${selectedList.length} paket avregistrerade som hyvlade.`
            : `Spec #${id} bekräftad — ${selectedList.length} paket avregistrerade.`
          });
        },
        waitForRender: waitForDocumentRender,
        print: printSpec,
      });
      if (result.status === "printed" && !specId) {
        navigate(`${basePath}/${result.id}`);
      }
    } catch (e: unknown) {
      toast({
        title: (e instanceof Error ? e.message : null) ?? "Fel vid bekräftelse",
        variant: "destructive",
      });
    } finally {
      confirmInFlightRef.current = false;
      setConfirming(false);
    }
  }

  async function ateroppen() {
    if (!specId) return;
    setAteropping(true);
    try {
      const data = await customFetch<{ ateroppnade: number }>(`/api/spec/${specId}/ateroppen`, { method: "POST" });
      setStatus("utkast");
      invalidateLagerCache();
      toast({ title: "Spec återöppnad", description: `${data.ateroppnade} paket återförda till lagret.` });
    } catch {
      toast({ title: "Fel vid återöppning", variant: "destructive" });
    } finally {
      setAteropping(false);
    }
  }

  async function deleteSpec() {
    if (!specId) return;
    setDeleting(true);
    try {
      await customFetch(`/api/spec/${specId}`, { method: "DELETE" });
      invalidateLagerCache();
      toast({ title: `${isHyvling ? "Hyvlingsorder" : "Spec"} #${displayNr} raderad` });
      navigate(basePath);
    } catch (e) {
      const msg = e instanceof ApiError
        ? e.message.replace(/^HTTP \d+ \w+: /, "")
        : "Okänt fel";
      toast({ title: "Fel vid radering", description: msg, variant: "destructive" });
    } finally {
      setDeleting(false);
    }
  }

  async function konverteraTillHyvling() {
    if (!specId) return;
    setKonverterar(true);
    try {
      await customFetch(`/api/spec/${specId}/konvertera-till-hyvling`, { method: "POST" });
      invalidateLagerCache();
      toast({ title: "Spec flyttad till Hyvlingsorder" });
      navigate(`/hyvling/${specId}`);
    } catch (e: unknown) {
      const msg = e instanceof Error ? e.message : "Okänt fel";
      toast({ title: "Fel vid konvertering", description: msg, variant: "destructive" });
    } finally {
      setKonverterar(false);
    }
  }

  async function konverteraTillImpregnering() {
    if (!specId) return;
    setKonvertararTillImp(true);
    try {
      await customFetch(`/api/spec/${specId}/konvertera-till-impregnering`, { method: "POST" });
      invalidateLagerCache();
      toast({ title: "Spec flyttad till Impregnering" });
      navigate(`/impregnering/${specId}`);
    } catch (e: unknown) {
      const msg = e instanceof Error ? e.message : "Okänt fel";
      toast({ title: "Fel vid konvertering", description: msg, variant: "destructive" });
    } finally {
      setKonvertararTillImp(false);
    }
  }

  const today = new Date().toLocaleDateString("sv-SE", { day: "numeric", month: "long", year: "numeric" });
  const confirmed = status === "bekraftad";

  function previewSpec() {
    previewDocument({
      sourceId: "spec-print",
      title: `${isHyvling ? "Hyvlingsorder" : "Specifikation"} – förhandsgranskning`,
      orientation: isHyvling ? "landscape" : "portrait",
    });
  }

  async function printSpec() {
    try {
      await printDocument({ sourceId: "spec-print", title: "Specifikation" });
    } catch (error) {
      toast({
        title: "Kunde inte förbereda utskriften",
        description: error instanceof Error ? error.message : undefined,
        variant: "destructive",
      });
    }
  }

  // Empty the dedicated print root when navigating away.
  useEffect(() => {
    return () => {
      cleanupDocumentPrint();
    };
  }, []);

  const printSpecRef = useRef(printSpec);
  useEffect(() => { printSpecRef.current = printSpec; });
  useEffect(() => {
    const h = () => printSpecRef.current();
    window.addEventListener("shortcut:print", h);
    return () => window.removeEventListener("shortcut:print", h);
  }, []);

  async function downloadPdfSpec() {
    setPdfLoading(true);
    try {
      const nr = displayNr ?? specId ?? "utkast";
      await downloadDocumentPdf({
        sourceId: "spec-print",
        title: isHyvling ? "Hyvlingsorder" : "Specifikation",
        fileName: `${isHyvling ? "Hyvlingsorder" : "Spec"}-${nr}${kund ? `-${kund}` : ""}.pdf`,
        orientation: isHyvling ? "landscape" : "portrait",
      });
    } catch (e) {
      toast({ title: "PDF-export misslyckades", description: e instanceof Error ? e.message : "Okänt fel", variant: "destructive" });
    } finally {
      setPdfLoading(false);
    }
  }

  function exportToExcel() {
    const nr = displayNr ?? specId ?? "utkast";
    const typ = isHyvling ? "Hyvlingsorder" : "Spec";
    const fileName = `${typ}-${nr}${kund ? `-${kund}` : ""}.xlsx`;

    const infoRows = [
      [typ, `#${nr}`],
      ["Kund", kund || "—"],
      ["Datum", today],
      ...(trailerNr ? [["Trailer", trailerNr]] : []),
      ...(orderNr ? [["Order", orderNr]] : []),
      [],
    ];

    const headers = [
      "Paket Nr", "Träslag", "Profil",
      "Nom T (mm)", "Nom B (mm)", "Act T (mm)", "Act B (mm)",
      "Längd (dm)", "Styck", "Lpm",
      "Nom m³", "Act m³",
      ...(hasPris ? ["Pris/m³", "Summa (kr)"] : []),
    ];

    const dataRows = selectedList.map(p => [
      p.pktNr ?? "",
      p.traslag ?? "",
      p.profil ?? "",
      p.nomTjocklek ?? "",
      p.nomBredd ?? "",
      p.actTjocklek ?? "",
      p.actBredd ?? "",
      p.langdDm ?? "",
      p.antal ?? "",
      p.lpm != null ? +p.lpm.toFixed(1) : "",
      computedNomM3(p) != null ? +((computedNomM3(p) as number).toFixed(3)) : "",
      computedActM3(p) != null ? +((computedActM3(p) as number).toFixed(3)) : "",
      ...(hasPris ? [p.saljPris ?? "", p.saljSum ?? ""] : []),
    ]);

    const totalRow = [
      `TOTALT — ${selectedList.length} paket`, "", "",
      "", "", "", "",
      "", totals.styck, +totals.lpm.toFixed(1),
      +totals.nomM3.toFixed(3), +totals.actM3.toFixed(3),
      ...(hasPris ? ["", +totals.saljSum.toFixed(2)] : []),
    ];

    const allRows = [...infoRows, headers, ...dataRows, totalRow];
    const ws = XLSX.utils.aoa_to_sheet(allRows);

    const headerRowIdx = infoRows.length;
    const range = XLSX.utils.decode_range(ws["!ref"] ?? "A1");

    for (let C = range.s.c; C <= range.e.c; C++) {
      const headerCell = XLSX.utils.encode_cell({ r: headerRowIdx, c: C });
      if (ws[headerCell]) {
        ws[headerCell].s = { font: { bold: true }, fill: { fgColor: { rgb: "DDDDDD" }, patternType: "solid" } };
      }
      const totalCell = XLSX.utils.encode_cell({ r: headerRowIdx + dataRows.length + 1, c: C });
      if (ws[totalCell]) {
        ws[totalCell].s = { font: { bold: true } };
      }
    }

    ws["!cols"] = [
      { wch: 10 }, { wch: 10 }, { wch: 14 },
      { wch: 10 }, { wch: 10 }, { wch: 10 }, { wch: 10 },
      { wch: 10 }, { wch: 8 }, { wch: 8 },
      { wch: 10 }, { wch: 10 },
      ...(hasPris ? [{ wch: 10 }, { wch: 12 }] : []),
    ];

    const wb = XLSX.utils.book_new();
    XLSX.utils.book_append_sheet(wb, ws, typ);
    XLSX.writeFile(wb, fileName);
  }

  if (loadingSpec) {
    return <div className="flex items-center gap-2 text-muted-foreground"><Loader2 className="h-4 w-4 animate-spin" /> Laddar spec...</div>;
  }

  return (
    <>
      <SkannaDialog
        open={skannaOpen}
        onClose={() => setSkannaOpen(false)}
        onPktNrFunnet={addPkgByPktNr}
        onOklarKod={handleOklarKodFranScanner}
        continuous
        selectedCount={selectedPackageCount}
        onFound={pkg => {
          if (pkg.pktNr) {
            void addPkgByPktNr(pkg.pktNr);
          } else {
            toast({ title: "Paketet saknar PKT-NR", variant: "destructive" });
          }
        }}
      />

      <style>{`
        @keyframes scan {
          0% { top: 0; }
          100% { top: 100%; }
        }
      `}</style>

      <div className="space-y-4">
        <div className="flex items-center justify-between gap-3 flex-wrap">
          <div className="flex items-center gap-3">
            <button onClick={() => navigate(basePath)} className="text-muted-foreground hover:text-foreground">
              <ArrowLeft className="h-5 w-5" />
            </button>
            <div>
              <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
                <h1 className="text-3xl font-bold tracking-tight">
                  {isHyvling
                    ? (displayNr ? `Hyvlingsorder #${displayNr}` : "Ny Hyvlingsorder")
                    : (displayNr ? `Spec #${displayNr}` : "Ny Specifikation")}
                </h1>
                {confirmed ? (
                  <Badge className={isHyvling
                    ? "bg-orange-100 text-orange-800 border-orange-200"
                    : "bg-green-100 text-green-800 border-green-200"
                  }>
                    {isHyvling ? "Hyvlad" : "Bekräftad"}
                  </Badge>
                ) : (
                  <Badge variant="outline">Utkast</Badge>
                )}
              </div>
              <p className="text-muted-foreground">
                {isHyvling
                  ? (confirmed
                    ? "Paketen är avregistrerade som sågade. Du kan återöppna om något behöver ändras."
                    : "Välj sågade paket som ska hyvlas — de avregistreras ur lagret som sågat.")
                  : (confirmed
                    ? "Paketen är avregistrerade. Du kan fortfarande skriva ut eller återöppna."
                    : "Välj paket och bekräfta för att avregistrera dem.")}
              </p>
            </div>
          </div>

          {specId && (
            <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
              {confirmed && (
                <AlertDialog>
                  <AlertDialogTrigger asChild>
                    <Button variant="outline" size="sm" className="gap-2" disabled={ateropping}>
                      {ateropping ? <Loader2 className="h-4 w-4 animate-spin" /> : <RotateCcw className="h-4 w-4" />}
                      Återöppna
                    </Button>
                  </AlertDialogTrigger>
                  <AlertDialogContent>
                    <AlertDialogHeader>
                      <AlertDialogTitle>Återöppna {isHyvling ? "Hyvlingsorder" : "Spec"} #{displayNr}?</AlertDialogTitle>
                      <AlertDialogDescription>
                        {isHyvling
                          ? "Ordern ändras tillbaka till utkast och paketen återförs till lagret som sågade (datum ut rensas)."
                          : "Specifikationen ändras tillbaka till utkast och alla paket i den återförs till lagret (datum ut rensas)."}
                        {" "}Du kan sedan redigera och bekräfta igen.
                      </AlertDialogDescription>
                    </AlertDialogHeader>
                    <AlertDialogFooter>
                      <AlertDialogCancel>Avbryt</AlertDialogCancel>
                      <AlertDialogAction onClick={ateroppen}>Återöppna</AlertDialogAction>
                    </AlertDialogFooter>
                  </AlertDialogContent>
                </AlertDialog>
              )}
              {!isHyvling && !confirmed && (
                <AlertDialog>
                  <AlertDialogTrigger asChild>
                    <Button variant="outline" size="sm" className="gap-2 text-amber-700 border-amber-300 hover:bg-amber-50" disabled={konverterar}>
                      {konverterar ? <Loader2 className="h-4 w-4 animate-spin" /> : <ArrowRightLeft className="h-4 w-4" />}
                      Flytta till Hyvlingsorder
                    </Button>
                  </AlertDialogTrigger>
                  <AlertDialogContent>
                    <AlertDialogHeader>
                      <AlertDialogTitle>Flytta Spec #{displayNr} till Hyvlingsorder?</AlertDialogTitle>
                      <AlertDialogDescription>
                        Specan konverteras till en hyvlingsorder och får ett nytt hyvlingsnummer. Paketen i specan påverkas inte. Du hittar den sedan under Hyvlingsordrar.
                      </AlertDialogDescription>
                    </AlertDialogHeader>
                    <AlertDialogFooter>
                      <AlertDialogCancel>Avbryt</AlertDialogCancel>
                      <AlertDialogAction onClick={konverteraTillHyvling}>Flytta</AlertDialogAction>
                    </AlertDialogFooter>
                  </AlertDialogContent>
                </AlertDialog>
              )}
              {!isHyvling && !confirmed && (
                <AlertDialog>
                  <AlertDialogTrigger asChild>
                    <Button variant="outline" size="sm" className="gap-2 text-blue-700 border-blue-300 hover:bg-blue-50" disabled={konvertararTillImp}>
                      {konvertararTillImp ? <Loader2 className="h-4 w-4 animate-spin" /> : <ArrowRightLeft className="h-4 w-4" />}
                      Flytta till Impregnering
                    </Button>
                  </AlertDialogTrigger>
                  <AlertDialogContent>
                    <AlertDialogHeader>
                      <AlertDialogTitle>Flytta Spec #{displayNr} till Impregnering?</AlertDialogTitle>
                      <AlertDialogDescription>
                        Specan konverteras till en impregneringsorder. Paketen påverkas inte. Du hittar den sedan under Impregnering.
                      </AlertDialogDescription>
                    </AlertDialogHeader>
                    <AlertDialogFooter>
                      <AlertDialogCancel>Avbryt</AlertDialogCancel>
                      <AlertDialogAction onClick={konverteraTillImpregnering}>Flytta</AlertDialogAction>
                    </AlertDialogFooter>
                  </AlertDialogContent>
                </AlertDialog>
              )}
              <AlertDialog>
                <AlertDialogTrigger asChild>
                  <Button variant="outline" size="sm" className="gap-2 text-destructive hover:bg-destructive/10 hover:text-destructive border-destructive/30" disabled={deleting}>
                    {deleting ? <Loader2 className="h-4 w-4 animate-spin" /> : <Trash2 className="h-4 w-4" />}
                    Radera spec
                  </Button>
                </AlertDialogTrigger>
                <AlertDialogContent>
                  <AlertDialogHeader>
                    <AlertDialogTitle>Radera {isHyvling ? "Hyvlingsorder" : "Spec"} #{displayNr}?</AlertDialogTitle>
                    <AlertDialogDescription asChild>
                      <div>
                        {confirmed ? (
                          <div className="space-y-2">
                            <p className="text-destructive font-medium">Ordern är bekräftad och kan inte raderas direkt.</p>
                            <p>Klicka på <strong>Återöppna</strong> för att öppna ordern igen — sedan kan du radera den. Tänk på att paketen återförs till lagret vid återöppning.</p>
                          </div>
                        ) : (
                          <p>Specifikationen raderas permanent. Paketen påverkas inte.</p>
                        )}
                      </div>
                    </AlertDialogDescription>
                  </AlertDialogHeader>
                  <AlertDialogFooter>
                    <AlertDialogCancel>Stäng</AlertDialogCancel>
                    {!confirmed && (
                      <AlertDialogAction
                        className="bg-destructive text-destructive-foreground hover:bg-destructive/90"
                        onClick={deleteSpec}
                      >
                        Radera
                      </AlertDialogAction>
                    )}
                  </AlertDialogFooter>
                </AlertDialogContent>
              </AlertDialog>
            </div>
          )}
        </div>

        {/* Document header preview */}
        <div className="rounded-lg border bg-white shadow-sm overflow-hidden">
          <div className="flex items-start justify-between px-6 py-4 border-b-2 border-[#2d5a27]">
            <div className="flex items-center gap-3">
              {logoSrc && <img src={logoSrc} alt={companyName || "Logotyp"} className="h-10 w-auto" />}
              <div>
                <div className="font-bold text-[#2d5a27] text-sm tracking-wide">{companyName}</div>
                <div className="text-xs text-gray-500 leading-relaxed mt-0.5">
                  {senderLocation && <div>{senderLocation}</div>}
                  {(documentSender.phone || documentSender.email) && (
                    <div>
                      {[documentSender.phone ? `Tel: ${documentSender.phone}` : "", documentSender.email]
                        .filter(Boolean)
                        .join(" · ")}
                    </div>
                  )}
                </div>
              </div>
            </div>
            <div className="text-center flex-1 px-6 pt-1">
              <div className="text-lg font-bold tracking-[3px] text-[#2d5a27]">
                {isHyvling ? "HYVLINGSORDER" : "SPECIFIKATION"}
              </div>
              {displayNr && <div className="text-xs text-gray-400 mt-0.5">#{displayNr}</div>}
            </div>
            <div className="text-right text-xs text-gray-600 leading-relaxed">
              <div><span className="text-gray-400">Datum:</span> <strong>{today}</strong></div>
              {!isHyvling && <div><span className="text-gray-400">Trailer:</span> <strong>{trailerNr || "—"}</strong></div>}
              {!isHyvling && <div><span className="text-gray-400">Ordernr:</span> <strong>{orderNr || "—"}</strong></div>}
              {!isHyvling && adress && (
                <div className="flex gap-1">
                  <span className="text-gray-400 shrink-0">Adress:</span>
                  <strong className="whitespace-pre-line text-left">{adress}</strong>
                </div>
              )}
            </div>
          </div>
          <div className="px-6 py-2 text-xs text-gray-500">
            <span className="text-gray-400 mr-2">{isHyvling ? "Anteckning:" : "Köpare:"}</span>
            <strong className="text-gray-700">{kund || "—"}</strong>
          </div>
        </div>

        <div className="grid grid-cols-1 lg:grid-cols-3 gap-4">
          {/* Left column */}
          <div className="lg:col-span-1 space-y-4">
            <Card>
              <CardHeader className="pb-3">
                <CardTitle className="text-base">Rubrikinfo</CardTitle>
              </CardHeader>
              <CardContent className="space-y-2">
                <div>
                  <label className="text-sm font-medium text-muted-foreground">
                    {isHyvling ? "Anteckning" : "Köpare"}
                  </label>
                  <Input
                    value={kund}
                    onChange={e => setKund(e.target.value)}
                    placeholder={isHyvling ? "Valfri anteckning" : "Köparens namn"}
                    disabled={confirmed}
                    list={isHyvling ? undefined : "kund-forslag"}
                  />
                  {!isHyvling && (
                    <datalist id="kund-forslag">
                      {kunder.map(k => <option key={k.id} value={k.namn} />)}
                    </datalist>
                  )}
                </div>
                {!isHyvling && (
                  <>
                    <div>
                      <label className="text-sm font-medium text-muted-foreground">Adress</label>
                      <Textarea
                        value={adress}
                        onChange={e => setAdress(e.target.value)}
                        placeholder={"Leveransadress\nPostnummer Ort"}
                        disabled={confirmed}
                        rows={2}
                        className="resize-none"
                      />
                    </div>
                    <div className="space-y-1">
                      <label className="text-sm font-medium text-muted-foreground">Trailer-nr</label>
                      <Input value={trailerNr} onChange={e => setTrailerNr(e.target.value)} placeholder="Trailer-nummer" disabled={confirmed} />
                    </div>
                    <div>
                      <label className="text-sm font-medium text-muted-foreground">Ordernummer</label>
                      <Input value={orderNr} onChange={e => setOrderNr(e.target.value)} placeholder="Ordernummer" disabled={confirmed} />
                    </div>
                    <div>
                      <label className="text-sm font-medium text-muted-foreground">Er referens</label>
                      <Input value={erReferens} onChange={e => setErReferens(e.target.value)} placeholder="Kundens kontakt eller referens" disabled={confirmed} />
                    </div>
                    <div>
                      <label className="text-sm font-medium text-muted-foreground">Anteckning</label>
                      <Textarea
                        value={anteckning}
                        onChange={e => setAnteckning(e.target.value)}
                        placeholder="Transport- eller kundanteckning"
                        disabled={confirmed}
                        rows={3}
                      />
                    </div>
                    <div>
                      <label className="text-sm font-medium text-muted-foreground">Certifikat på dokument</label>
                      <div className="mt-1.5 flex flex-col gap-2">
                        <label className="flex items-center gap-2 cursor-pointer select-none">
                          <Checkbox checked={showCertificationOnDocument} onCheckedChange={checked => setShowCertificationOnDocument(checked === true)} disabled={confirmed} />
                          <span className="text-sm font-medium">Visa certifieringsuppgifter på dokumentet</span>
                        </label>
                        {CERT_OPTIONS.map(cert => (
                          <label key={cert.id} className="flex items-center gap-2 cursor-pointer select-none">
                            <Checkbox
                              checked={certifikat.includes(cert.id)}
                              onCheckedChange={checked => {
                                setCertifikat(prev =>
                                  checked ? [...prev, cert.id] : prev.filter(c => c !== cert.id)
                                );
                              }}
                              disabled={confirmed}
                            />
                            <span className="text-sm font-medium">{cert.label}</span>
                            <span className="text-xs text-muted-foreground">{cert.nr}</span>
                          </label>
                        ))}
                      </div>
                    </div>
                  </>
                )}

                {/* Kopplad spec — link to related spec (e.g. sågat → hyvlat) */}
                <div>
                  <label className="text-sm font-medium text-muted-foreground">Kopplad spec</label>
                  {kopplatSpecId && kopplatSpec ? (
                    <div className="flex items-center gap-2 mt-1">
                      <a
                        href={kopplatSpec.typ === "hyvling" ? `/hyvling/${kopplatSpec.id}` : `/spec/${kopplatSpec.id}`}
                        className="text-sm font-medium text-primary hover:underline flex-1"
                        onClick={(e) => { e.preventDefault(); navigate(kopplatSpec.typ === "hyvling" ? `/hyvling/${kopplatSpec.id}` : `/spec/${kopplatSpec.id}`); }}
                      >
                        {specLabel(kopplatSpec)}
                      </a>
                      {!confirmed && (
                        <Button variant="ghost" size="sm" className="h-7 px-2 text-muted-foreground hover:text-destructive" onClick={() => { setKopplatSpecId(null); setKopplatSpec(null); }}>
                          <X className="h-3.5 w-3.5" />
                        </Button>
                      )}
                    </div>
                  ) : !confirmed ? (
                    <div className="mt-1 space-y-1">
                      {!specPickerOpen ? (
                        <Button variant="outline" size="sm" className="w-full gap-2 text-muted-foreground border-dashed" onClick={() => setSpecPickerOpen(true)}>
                          <Plus className="h-3.5 w-3.5" /> Koppla till en spec...
                        </Button>
                      ) : (
                        <div className="border rounded-lg p-2 space-y-2">
                          <div className="flex gap-2">
                            <Input
                              className="h-8 text-sm flex-1"
                              placeholder="Sök spec (kund, nummer...)"
                              value={specSokQuery}
                              onChange={e => setSpecSokQuery(e.target.value)}
                              autoFocus
                            />
                            <Button variant="ghost" size="sm" className="h-8 px-2" onClick={() => { setSpecPickerOpen(false); setSpecSokQuery(""); }}>
                              <X className="h-3.5 w-3.5" />
                            </Button>
                          </div>
                          <div className="max-h-48 overflow-y-auto space-y-1">
                            {alleSpecs
                              .filter(s => s.id !== specId && (
                                !specSokQuery ||
                                specLabel(s).toLowerCase().includes(specSokQuery.toLowerCase()) ||
                                String(s.id).includes(specSokQuery) ||
                                (s.hyvlingNr && String(s.hyvlingNr).includes(specSokQuery))
                              ))
                              .slice(0, 20)
                              .map(s => (
                                <button
                                  key={s.id}
                                  className="w-full text-left text-sm px-2 py-1.5 rounded hover:bg-muted flex items-center justify-between gap-2"
                                  onClick={() => {
                                    setKopplatSpecId(s.id);
                                    setKopplatSpec({ id: s.id, typ: s.typ, hyvlingNr: s.hyvlingNr, specNr: s.specNr, kund: s.kund, status: s.status });
                                    setSpecPickerOpen(false);
                                    setSpecSokQuery("");
                                  }}
                                >
                                  <span>{specLabel(s)}</span>
                                  <span className="text-xs text-muted-foreground shrink-0">{s.antalPaket} pkt</span>
                                </button>
                              ))}
                            {alleSpecs.filter(s => s.id !== specId).length === 0 && (
                              <p className="text-sm text-muted-foreground text-center py-2">Inga specs hittades</p>
                            )}
                          </div>
                        </div>
                      )}
                    </div>
                  ) : (
                    <p className="text-sm text-muted-foreground mt-1">—</p>
                  )}
                </div>

                {/* Reverse links — other specs that link to this one */}
                {reverseLinks.length > 0 && (
                  <div>
                    <label className="text-sm font-medium text-muted-foreground">Länkad från</label>
                    <div className="mt-1 space-y-1">
                      {reverseLinks.map(s => (
                        <a
                          key={s.id}
                          href={s.typ === "hyvling" ? `/hyvling/${s.id}` : `/spec/${s.id}`}
                          className="block text-sm font-medium text-primary hover:underline"
                          onClick={(e) => { e.preventDefault(); navigate(s.typ === "hyvling" ? `/hyvling/${s.id}` : `/spec/${s.id}`); }}
                        >
                          {specLabel(s)}
                        </a>
                      ))}
                    </div>
                  </div>
                )}
              </CardContent>
            </Card>

            {!confirmed && (
              <Card>
                <CardHeader className="pb-3">
                  <CardTitle className="text-base">Lägg till paket</CardTitle>
                </CardHeader>
                <CardContent className="space-y-2">
                  <Button
                    variant="outline"
                    className="w-full gap-2 border-dashed"
                    onClick={() => setSkannaOpen(true)}
                  >
                    <ScanLine className="h-4 w-4" />
                    Skanna paketlapp
                  </Button>
                  <div className="relative">
                    <Search className="absolute left-2.5 top-2.5 h-4 w-4 text-muted-foreground" />
                    <Input
                      className="pl-9"
                      placeholder="Sök pkt-nr, dim, varuslag, profil..."
                      value={search}
                      onChange={e => setSearch(e.target.value)}
                    />
                  </div>
                  <div className="max-h-64 overflow-y-auto space-y-1">
                    {searchPkgs.length === 0 && debouncedSearch.length > 0 && (
                      <p className="text-sm text-muted-foreground text-center py-4">Inga träffar</p>
                    )}
                    {searchPkgs.map(p => {
                      const isAdded = selected.has(p.id);
                      return (
                        <div key={p.id} className="flex items-center justify-between gap-2 p-2 rounded border text-sm hover:bg-muted/50">
                          <div className="min-w-0 text-xs">
                            <span className="font-mono font-medium">{p.pktNr ?? "-"}</span>
                            <span className="text-muted-foreground ml-2">{p.varuslag} {dimStr(p.nomTjocklek, p.nomBredd)} {p.langdDm}dm</span>
                            {p.profil && <span className="text-muted-foreground ml-1">· {p.profil}</span>}
                            {p.plats && <span className="text-muted-foreground ml-1">· {p.plats}</span>}
                          </div>
                          <Button
                            size="sm"
                            variant={isAdded ? "secondary" : "default"}
                            className="h-7 px-2 shrink-0"
                            onClick={() => isAdded ? removePkg(p.id) : addPkg(p)}
                          >
                            {isAdded ? <X className="h-3 w-3" /> : <Plus className="h-3 w-3" />}
                          </Button>
                        </div>
                      );
                    })}
                  </div>
                </CardContent>
              </Card>
            )}
          </div>

          {/* Right column */}
          <div className="lg:col-span-2 space-y-4">
            <Card>
              <CardHeader className="pb-3 flex flex-col sm:flex-row sm:items-center gap-2 sm:gap-0 sm:justify-between">
                <CardTitle className="text-base">
                  Valda paket
                  {selectedList.length > 0 && (
                    <Badge variant="secondary" className="ml-2">{selectedList.length} st</Badge>
                  )}
                </CardTitle>
                <div className="flex flex-wrap items-center gap-2">
                  {specId && !confirmed && (
                    <Popover open={prislistaPopoverOpen} onOpenChange={open => { if (open) oppnaPrislistaPopover(); else setPrislistaPopoverOpen(false); }}>
                      <PopoverTrigger asChild>
                        <Button
                          variant="outline"
                          size="sm"
                          className="gap-1.5 text-xs h-8"
                          disabled={tillampingPrislista || selectedList.length === 0}
                        >
                          {tillampingPrislista
                            ? <Loader2 className="h-3.5 w-3.5 animate-spin" />
                            : <RefreshCw className="h-3.5 w-3.5" />}
                          Tillämpa prislista
                          <ChevronDown className="h-3 w-3 opacity-50" />
                        </Button>
                      </PopoverTrigger>
                      <PopoverContent className="w-72 p-3" align="end">
                        <p className="text-xs font-medium mb-2">Välj prislista att tillämpa</p>
                        {laderPrislistor ? (
                          <div className="flex items-center gap-2 text-xs text-muted-foreground py-2">
                            <Loader2 className="h-3.5 w-3.5 animate-spin" /> Laddar prislistor…
                          </div>
                        ) : allaPrislistor.length === 0 ? (
                          <p className="text-xs text-muted-foreground py-2">Inga prislistor hittades.</p>
                        ) : (
                          <div className="flex flex-col gap-1 max-h-56 overflow-y-auto mb-3">
                            {allaPrislistor.map(pl => (
                              <button
                                key={pl.id}
                                onClick={() => setValdPrislistaId(pl.id)}
                                className={`flex items-start gap-2 rounded px-2 py-1.5 text-left text-xs transition-colors ${valdPrislistaId === pl.id ? "bg-primary text-primary-foreground" : "hover:bg-muted"}`}
                              >
                                <div className="flex-1 min-w-0">
                                  <div className="font-medium truncate">{pl.namn}</div>
                                  {pl.kund && <div className={`truncate ${valdPrislistaId === pl.id ? "text-primary-foreground/70" : "text-muted-foreground"}`}>{pl.kund}{pl.datum ? ` · ${pl.datum}` : ""}</div>}
                                </div>
                                {valdPrislistaId === pl.id && <CheckCircle className="h-3.5 w-3.5 shrink-0 mt-0.5" />}
                              </button>
                            ))}
                          </div>
                        )}
                        <Button
                          size="sm"
                          className="w-full text-xs h-8"
                          disabled={!valdPrislistaId || laderPrislistor}
                          onClick={tillampPrislista}
                        >
                          <RefreshCw className="h-3.5 w-3.5 mr-1.5" />
                          Tillämpa
                        </Button>
                      </PopoverContent>
                    </Popover>
                  )}
                  {hasPris && (
                    <label className="flex items-center gap-1.5 text-sm cursor-pointer select-none">
                      <input
                        type="checkbox"
                        checked={visaPrisIPrint}
                        onChange={e => setVisaPrisIPrint(e.target.checked)}
                        className="h-4 w-4 rounded border"
                      />
                      Visa pris
                    </label>
                  )}
                  {confirmed ? (
                    <>
                      <Button variant="outline" onClick={downloadPdfSpec} disabled={pdfLoading} className="gap-2">
                        {pdfLoading ? <Loader2 className="h-4 w-4 animate-spin" /> : <FileDown className="h-4 w-4" />}
                        {pdfLoading ? "Genererar PDF…" : "Ladda ner PDF"}
                      </Button>
                      <Button variant="outline" onClick={exportToExcel} className="gap-2">
                        <Sheet className="h-4 w-4" /> Exportera Excel
                      </Button>
                      <Button variant="outline" onClick={printSpec} className="gap-2">
                        <Printer className="h-4 w-4" /> Skriv ut igen
                      </Button>
                    </>
                  ) : (
                    <>
                      <Button variant="outline" onClick={previewSpec} disabled={selectedList.length === 0} className="gap-2">
                        <Printer className="h-4 w-4" /> Förhandsvisa
                      </Button>
                      <Button variant="outline" onClick={saveSpec} disabled={saving || selectedList.length === 0} className="gap-2">
                        {saving ? <Loader2 className="h-4 w-4 animate-spin" /> : <Save className="h-4 w-4" />}
                        Spara utkast
                      </Button>
                      <Button
                        onClick={confirmSpec}
                        disabled={confirming || selectedList.length === 0}
                        className={`gap-2 ${isHyvling ? "bg-orange-600 hover:bg-orange-700" : "bg-green-700 hover:bg-green-800"}`}
                      >
                        {confirming ? <Loader2 className="h-4 w-4 animate-spin" /> : <CheckCircle className="h-4 w-4" />}
                        Bekräfta & skriv ut
                      </Button>
                    </>
                  )}
                </div>
              </CardHeader>
              <CardContent>
                {kollisioner.size > 0 && !confirmed && (
                  <div className="flex items-start gap-2 rounded-md border border-amber-300 bg-amber-50 dark:bg-amber-950/30 dark:border-amber-800 px-3 py-2 mb-3 text-sm text-amber-800 dark:text-amber-300">
                    <AlertTriangle className="h-4 w-4 mt-0.5 shrink-0 text-amber-600" />
                    <span>
                      <strong>{kollisioner.size} paket</strong> i denna spec finns även på andra specifikationer — markerade med <AlertTriangle className="inline h-3.5 w-3.5 text-amber-600" /> i listan. Hovra på ikonen för att se vilken spec.
                    </span>
                  </div>
                )}
                {selectedList.length === 0 ? (
                  <p className="text-sm text-muted-foreground text-center py-8">
                    {confirmed ? "Inga paket i denna spec." : "Sök och lägg till paket till vänster."}
                  </p>
                ) : (
                  <div className="overflow-x-auto -mx-4 sm:mx-0">
                    <table className="text-sm border-collapse min-w-full w-max sm:w-full">
                      <thead>
                        <tr className="border-b">
                          {(["pktNr","traslag","nomDim","langdDm","antal","m3","actM3"] as SpecSortKey[]).map((key, i) => {
